# Reconstructions from primary dimensional facts

The public-domain imagery rule still applies to every image used in a model. A structure
can also be reconstructed from a sufficiently specific primary written description and layout,
without using architectural imagery. Search Qualla first and retain the inventory; leave
questionable photographs unused. A factual reconstruction makes no photographic-fidelity claim.

The supported cases include Allan Cunningham Monument, heritage entry 650225. The
authority specifies its approximately five-metre height, three-metre square base, stone obelisk,
stepped rendered base and low enclosure. The source bundle records the actual cached primary
text extraction, independent native memorial identity, measured controls and estimated details.
Taper, subdivisions, stone pattern, plaque and enclosure dimensions are estimates. It represents
the documented 2022 structure and is not certified for automatic current-world replacement.

Maputo–Katembe Bridge has a separate engineering case. It binds the seven original elevated
road/approach paths and all 133 native controls, the 680m main span, 141.2m tower height, 60m
navigation clearance, 25.6m deck width and 3m steel box depth. The primary technical description
specifies the suspension system and concrete approach construction. Portal sections, cable
sag and hanger count, piers, finish and grades remain estimates. The published approximate
total and mapped road lengths use different boundaries; native paths are preserved without
stretching them to a reported total. No source photographs or diagrams are used.

Andenes Lighthouse binds the owner's construction record (page 14) to the exact native
Q4753689 tower part and enclosing heritage-building outline, with 19 controls each. The owner
specifies a 40m red cast-iron tower, two exterior iron galleries one storey apart, lantern,
conical metal roof, concrete foundation/steps, teak door and cast-iron window frames. Both
mapped outlines are retained; their vertical roles, upper taper and section divisions, minor
fittings, window census and finishes are explicit estimates. Detached buildings are separate
structures. No source photographs, figures or drawings are used.

Sorpe Dam binds the original mapped main dam, full crest paths and connected curved cascade
to the owner's technical table and annual report. The table specifies the 69m core-foundation
and 60m valley heights, 307m greatest foot width, 700m crest length and 10m crown width. The
report specifies 37 masonry spillway basins, 66m drop and widths increasing from 3m to 7m.
Original mapped lengths are preserved rather than stretched to rounded published totals.
Submerged/abutment profiles, absolute grades, basin partitions, fittings and finishes remain
estimates. The unchanged source elevation field has substantial offsets along the thin crest;
this is an approximate dated placement. Detached powerhouse buildings and the distant foredam
are separate structures. No source imagery is used.

Bosscha's Zeiss dome building uses the ordinary `bosscha_zeiss_dome` structure identity for
the fixed story target “The Tea Planter's Telescope.” Its owner specifies the 14.5m rotating
steel dome and 2mm sheet construction; the exact native main-building outline has 19 controls.
The complete defining main dome building and its base are included; other separate campus
buildings are excluded. Base/apron interpretation, wall height, dome profile, shutter pose,
door/windows and finishes are explicit estimates. All imagery, including the conflicted hero
and stamps, remains unused and unrepaired. No photographic fidelity is claimed.

Independence Memorial Hall binds its exact twelve-control native plan to official tourism and the principal architect’s 1953 construction description as traceably transmitted by a 2018 report. The complete concrete pavilion, raised three-tier terrace/basement exterior, 48 reported pillars, four corner towers, 28 frieze panels and two-pitched Kandyan roof are included. Vertical sections, grid spacing, roof profile, abstract original reliefs and symbolic lion motifs are explicit estimates. The source record distinguishes the transmitted account from an original 1953 page; no figures or architectural imagery are used.

Atacama Cosmology Telescope binds the complete eight-control native enclosure to the primary instrument publication and the designers’ numerical optical prescription. The full 12m instrument, 13m stationary screen, co-moving inner screens, both off-axis ellipsoidal mirror surfaces and receiver cabin are modeled. Mirror geometry follows Table 2 and Equation 1; its pose, screen profile/edge interpretation, mount, supports and cabin sections are explicit estimates. NASA hosting does not confer image rights: every source figure and photograph remains unused.

Bujagali binds the complete mapped composite dam, original 899m crest, integral open-air five-unit powerhouse and both spillways to three primary construction accounts. Reported 30m sections, three earthfill portions, five Kaplan units, two radial plus one flap gate, emergency siphon spillway and two stored wheel gates constrain the asset. Functional divisions, native boundary roles, floors, slopes, gate pose and equipment are declared estimates. Original terrain significantly obscures lower works and has a severe distant river anomaly; ground contact and vertical registration remain unverified. All imagery remains unused.

Serpentine Main Dam binds its full original 23-control physical boundary, five-control crest/bridge, ten-control enlarged spillway and two-control conduit to government 55m/424m/1961 construction facts and the heritage record of the 2001/2002 enlargement and demolition of the old gates/observation platform. The whole main dam and intrinsic control assembly are included; tower form/registration, sections and grades are explicit estimates. The original provider terrain contains a severe low void and obscures lower works; ground contact remains unverified. All imagery remains unused, and the later crest-raising proposal is not represented as completed work.

Markar Clock Tower binds its exact independent Q5902355 location to historian Hossein Masarrat’s own republished interview reporting 18m height, and the cultural complex’s written square-plan, four-clock-face and plain sloped-roof description. The full base/body/clock chamber/roof are modeled. The native point supplies no measured footprint; widths, tier divisions, dial details, original abstract inscription strokes, azimuth and finish are explicit estimates. Every image and video remains unused; neighboring campus buildings are separate.

Brunei Energy Hub binds the complete original four-control, two-storey former customs-house outline to the renovation designer’s written flat-roof/box-form, louvre and customs-signage account and founding-partner identity. Its genuine central full-depth vehicle passage follows the user-owned Qualla article. The designer account is an actual cached search extraction; its original URL now returns404, which is disclosed. All heights, openings/census, details, finish and grades are estimates. The original94.43m native building is retained without stretching to the rounded400-foot wharf narrative, and every image remains unused.

The readiness checker binds that exact case and its source bytes, Qualla search, identity and
numeric controls. This is not a general image exemption or permission to substitute a generic
building. New cases require their own sufficient primary specification and explicit verification.
Adding any reference photograph to this method fails its check. Photographic reference methods
retain their existing license and provenance checks unchanged.

Factual reconstructions still require complete physical scope, source/runtime verification, four
authored viewing levels, shared materials, personally inspected current renders, geographic and
original terrain review, and idempotent story annotations in the same authoring pass. Their QA
records `imageryReview.status: not-used` and a separate bound `primaryFactsReview`; they do not
count as public-domain photographic reference sets.

- `dr_xuma_house`: whole original Empilweni single-storey L-house and both complete roofs, curved bays, arched entry/plain columns, brick/plaster exterior and full bracketed canopy/stoep. Official South African Tourism construction facts plus explicitly transmitted Flo Bird 1991 architecture; original report not retrieved. Exact published house point and four original adjacent-street controls provide context only; no mapped house outline. All plan/roof/section/detail/finish estimates declared, with nominal 20% plan uncertainty. Qualla-first 10 families; all imagery unused. Original 128 m field/frame/floors/main sections retained; 43 current frames plus original field inspected; four LODs 384/2248/4044/7152, five validation groups and 20 actual mesh probes; article annotation written and repeat verified in the same pass. No photographic/survey/current replacement claim.
